### [101] [A Structure Sheaf for Kirch Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05515)
*Alexander Borisov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究在Kirch拓扑（一种Hausdorff、连通、局部连通的自然数拓扑）上新构造的局部LIP函数环层的基本性质，主要关注零阶、一阶上同调以及关于基本开集覆盖的Čech上同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kirch拓扑（1969年提出）是$\mathbb N$上一种Hausdorff、连通、局部连通的拓扑，类似于$\mathbb C$上的通常拓扑。尽管这一拓扑存在已久，但直到作者最近的工作之前，一直没有构建出类似于复解析函数层的Kirch拓扑类比物。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者在其最新论文中构造了局部LIP函数（locally LIP functions）的环层作为Kirch拓扑上的自然层。本文通过研究该层的基本性质，特别是零阶和一阶上同调，以及关于基本开集覆盖的Čech上同调，来探索这一新构造。

Result: 文章建立了局部LIP函数环层在Kirch拓扑上的基本上同调性质，包括零阶和一阶上同调的结果，以及关于基本开集覆盖的Čech上同调的相关结论。

Conclusion: 本文为Kirch拓扑上的局部LIP函数层建立了基础的上同调理论，为进一步研究这一新构造的层性质提供了起点。

Abstract: Kirch topology on $\mathbb N$ goes back to 1969, and is remarkable for being Hausdorff, connected, and locally connected. In this sense, it is analogous to the usual topology on $\mathbb C,$ yet, to the author's knowledge, there have been no Kirch topology analogs of the sheaf of complex-analytic functions until very recently. In our latest paper we constructed such natural sheaf of rings, the sheaf of locally LIP functions. In this paper we investigate some of its basic properties, primarily regarding zeroth and first cohomology and Cech cohomology with respect to covers by basic open sets.

</details>


### [102] [Multiple modular L-functions and modular iterated integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05672)
*Mahiro Yokomizo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文去除了Choie和Ihara关于模L-函数与模迭代积分之间关系的一个限制性假设（模形式的q-展开常数项为零），将结论推广到一般模形式，并证明了一个函数方程，推广了Choie-Ihara的结果，同时提供了显式计算实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Choie和Ihara在[3]中建立了多重模L-函数与模迭代积分之间的联系，但该结果受限于一个较强的假设——所有涉及的模形式在q-展开中的常数项必须为零。这一假设限制了理论的应用范围，因此本文旨在移除该假设，建立更一般性的关系。

Method: 本文通过放宽常数项为零的约束，直接处理带有非零常数项的一般模形式，重新建立模迭代积分与多重模L-函数之间的关系。同时，利用Brown的一般结果[2]中的一个特例，为模迭代积分的函数方程提供一个证明，并基于此推广Choie-Ihara的结论。最后通过显式实例计算验证框架的正确性。

Result: 成功去除了Choie-Ihara结果中的限制性假设，将多重模L-函数与模迭代积分之间的对应关系推广到一般模形式；给出了模迭代积分函数方程的一个证明；得到了Choie-Ihara结果的推广形式；通过显式实例复现了Brown的初步计算结果，验证了所提出框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的更一般框架完善了多重模L-函数与模迭代积分之间的理论联系，扩展了Choie和Ihara的工作，同时通过实例验证表明该框架具有实际计算价值。

Abstract: The connection between multiple modular L-functions, as defined by Manin in [5], and modular iterated integrals was made explicit by Choie and Ihara [3] under the restrictive assumption that all modular forms involved have vanishing constant terms in their q-expansions. In this paper, we remove the assumption and establish the relationship between modular iterated integrals and multiple modular L-functions for general modular forms, including those with nonzero constant terms. We also provide a proof of a functional equation for modular iterated integrals, which is a specialization of a general result obtained by Brown [2]. This leads us to a generalization of the result of Choie-Ihara [3]. In the final part of the paper, we compute explicit examples of modular iterated integrals. These calculations essentially reproduce the explicit initial computations carried out by Brown [2], but they also serve to validate the broader framework developed in this work.

</details>


### [103] [Rankin--Selberg coefficients in arithmetic progressions modulo prime powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05766)
*Tengyou Zhu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在假设GL₂ Maass形式的Ramanujan–Petersson猜想成立的前提下，证明了对于k≥2且p≠3的素数幂模数q=p^k，Rankin–Selberg系数{λ_f(n)^2}_{n≥1}在算术级数n ≡ a mod q中的分布水平可达到θ=2/5+3/305−ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Rankin–Selberg系数在算术级数中的分布性质，这是解析数论中关于模形式傅里叶系数分布问题的延伸，旨在获得更优的分布水平指数。

Method: 利用GL₂ Maass形式的Ramanujan–Petersson猜想作为假设条件，结合解析数论中的筛法或圆法等工具对Rankin–Selberg系数进行估计。

Result: 在指定条件下，得到了分布水平θ=2/5+3/305−ε，即θ≈0.4098，优于传统的1/2−ε界限。

Conclusion: 在强分析假设（Ramanujan–Petersson猜想）下，Rankin–Selberg系数的平方在素数幂模数算术级数中具有非平凡且超过2/5的分布水平。

Abstract: Let $\varepsilon>0$ be given. For prime power moduli $q=p^k$ with $k\geq 2$ and $p\neq 3$, and assuming the Ramanujan--Petersson conjecture for $\GL_2$ Maass forms, we prove that the Rankin--Selberg coefficients $\{λ_f(n)^2\}_{n\geq 1}$ have a level of distribution $θ=2/5+3/305-\varepsilon$ in arithmetic progressions $n \equiv a \bmod q$.

</details>


### [104] [The quotient problem for linear recurrence sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05784)
*Parvathi S Nair,S. S. Rout*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性递归序列比值取整的有限性问题，通过引入对数规模为o(n)的因子d_{m,n}，证明了在特定条件下存在多项式P使得d_{m,n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)为多重递归，而V(n)/P(n)为线性递归，利用Schmidt子空间定理及移动超平面、移动多项式和移动点等工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典丢番图问题关注线性递归序列比值U(n)/V(n)为整数的n的有限性，本文将其推广到二维参数空间(m,n)及更一般的代数码环境，研究存在对数规模亚线性增长因子d_{m,n}使得d_{m,n}U(m)/V(n)属于有限生成子环时解集的有限性问题。

Method: 运用Schmidt子空间定理，结合移动超平面、移动多项式和移动点的概念，通过构造特定的多项式P，分析d_{m,n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)和V(n)/P(n)的递归结构，区分m≠n和m=n两种情形分别证明。

Result: 当m≠n时，存在多项式P使得d_{m,n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)为多重递归且V(n)/P(n)为线性递归；当m=n时，两者均为线性递归。

Conclusion: 本文为线性递归序列比值为整数的有限性问题提供了更广泛的框架，将经典一维问题推广到二维情形并考虑了更一般的代数环结构。

Abstract: Let $\{U(m)\}_{m\in \N}$ and $\{V(n)\}_{n\in \N}$ be linear recurrence sequences. It is a well-known Diophantine problem to determine the finiteness of the set of natural numbers $n$ such that the ratio $U(n)/V(n)$ is an integer. We study the finiteness problem for the set $(m, n)\in \mathbb{N}^2$ such that there exist non-zero positive integers $d_{m, n}$ satisfying $\log |d_{m, n}|=o(n)$, and $d_{m, n}U(m)/V(n)$ is an element from a finitely generated subring of $\C$. In particular, we prove that for $m\neq n $, there exists a polynomial $P$ such that $d_{m, n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)$ is a multi-recurrence and $V(n)/P(n)$ is a linear recurrence and for $m=n$ both $d_{m, n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)$ and $V(n)/P(n)$ are linear recurrences. To prove our results, we employ Schmidt's subspace theorem, and the concept of moving hyperplanes, moving polynomials, and moving points.

</details>


### [105] [Evaluation of eight different families of cubic Euler sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06034)
*J. Braun,H. J. Bentz*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究次数为4、5、6的三次欧拉和，考虑三种不同分母类型，证明所有八种不同族系的非线性欧拉和均可通过zeta值和特定多对数函数值显式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展对三次欧拉和的理解，特别是高次（4、5、6次）情况下不同分母类型对应的完整族系求解问题。

Method: 考虑三种分母类型 $1/k^n$, $1/((2k-1)^n)$ 和 $1/(k(2k-1))$，系统研究八种不同族系的非线性欧拉和，利用zeta值和多对数函数值 $Li_4(1/2)$, $Li_5(1/2)$, $Li_6(1/2)$, $Li_6(-1/2)$, $Li_6(-1/8)$ 进行表达。

Result: 所有八种族系的非线性三次欧拉和均可用zeta值和特定多对数函数值显式计算。

Conclusion: 三次欧拉和在次数4、5、6下，三种分母类型的完整族系均可闭式求解，丰富了欧拉和的理论结果。

Abstract: We present a study on cubic Euler sums of degree four, five and six, where three different types of denominators $1/k^n$, $1/((2k-1)^n)$ and $1/(k(2k-1))$ will be considered We demonstrate that for all three orders the complete variety of corresponding nonlinear Euler sums belonging to the eight different families can be explicitly calculated in terms of zeta values and polylogarithmic values $Li_4(1/2)$, $Li_5(1/2)$, $Li_6(1/2)$, $Li_6(-1/2)$ and $Li_6(-1/8)$.

</details>


### [106] [$δ$-Badly approximable numbers and ubiquitously losing sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06325)
*Jimmy Tseng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了坏逼近数集的一个自然滤过 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ) \subset \boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ')$，证明了 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ 是 $(1/3, 18δ)$-获胜集并给出了其Hausdorff维数下界；引入了$(α, β)$- ubiquitous losing集的概念，证明了 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ 是 $(1/2, 18/δ)$-ubiquitously losing集，并给出了其严格小于1的Hausdorff维数上界；结合有限交性质和双Lipschitz转移性质，得到了 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ 平移集的有限交的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了补充好逼近数集被τ-好逼近数滤过的结构，构造坏逼近数集上的自然滤过，并进一步研究其赢集性质与分形维数。

Method: 利用Schmidt博弈理论，引入$(α, β)$- ubiquitous losing集的新概念，结合有限交性质和双Lipschitz转移性质进行分析。

Result: 证明了 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ 是 $(1/3, 18δ)$-获胜集；给出了其Hausdorff维数的下界；证明了它是 $(1/2, 18/δ)$-ubiquitously losing集，给出了严格小于1的Hausdorff维数上界；得到了平移集有限交的结果。

Conclusion: 通过引入新概念并运用Schmidt博弈，完善了坏逼近数集的滤过结构理论，给出了维数上下界估计，并推广到平移集有限交的情形。

Abstract: We construct a natural filtration $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ) \subset \boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ')$ for $δ\geq δ'>0$ on the set of badly approximable numbers to complement the filtration of the well approximable numbers by the $τ$-well approximable numbers. We show that the set $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ is a $(1/3, 18 δ)$-winning set and give a lower bound on its Hausdorff dimension. We introduce the notion of $(α, β)$-$\textit{ubiquitously losing sets}$ to the theory of Schmidt games, give an upper bound on the Hausdorff dimension of an $(α, β)$-ubiquitously losing set that is strictly less than full Hausdorff dimension, show that $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ is a $(1/2, 18/δ)$-ubiquitously losing set, and give an upper bound on the Hausdorff dimension of $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ that is strictly less than one. Combined with a finite intersection property and a bilipschitz transfer property, we obtain results for finite intersections of translates of $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$.

</details>


### [107] [A multidimensional Szemerédi theorem in integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06360)
*Jingwei Guo,Changxing Miao,Guoqing Zhan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意整数n≥2和严格递增的正整数n元组(m1,...,mn)，任何密度至少为(log N)^{-c}的子集A⊂[N]^n都包含形如x, x+r^{m1}e1, ..., x+r^{mn}en的非平凡配置，其中c是正常数。这一量化多维Szemerédi定理推广了Peluse、Prendiville和Shao关于(x,y),(x+r,y),(x,y+r^2)配置的二维结果，且该定理是通过一个有效的"流行"版本推导得到的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Peluse、Prendiville和Shao关于特定二维配置(x,y),(x+r,y),(x,y+r^2)的量化Szemerédi定理推广到高维情形，考虑更一般的增长指数序列(m1,...,mn)对应的配置。

Method: 通过建立有效的"流行"（popular）版本定理来推导主定理，利用密度至少为(log N)^{-c}的条件保证配置的存在性。

Result: 得到了量化多维Szemerédi定理：任意密度≥(log N)^{-c}的子集A⊂[N]^n必然包含形如x, x+r^{m1}e1, ..., x+r^{mn}en的非平凡配置。

Conclusion: 该结果将二维的量化结果成功推广到任意高维，并适用于更一般的增长指数序列。

Abstract: For any integer $n \geq 2$, let $(m_{1},\ldots,m_{n})$ be a strictly increasing $n$-tuple of positive integers. We show that any subset $A\subset [N]^n$ of density at least $(\log N)^{-c}$ contains a nontrivial configuration of the form \begin{equation*} \boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{x}+r^{m_{1}}\boldsymbol{e_{1}},\ldots,\boldsymbol{x}+r^{m_{n}}\boldsymbol{e_{n}}, \end{equation*} where $c=c(n,m_{1},\ldots,m_{n} )$ is a positive constant. This quantitative multidimensional Szemerédi theorem extends a recent two-dimensional result of Peluse, Prendiville, and Shao concerning the configuration of the form $(x,y),(x+r,y),\left(x,y+r^{2}\right)$. The theorem is obtained as a consequence of an effective ``popular'' version.

</details>


### [108] [On algebraic twists with composite moduli, II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06363)
*Yongxiao Lin,Philippe Michel*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究GL(3,Q)上自守系数的相关和与复合模迹函数之间的界限，是之前工作的延续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期工作基础上，将研究从简单模推广到复合模情形，探索更一般的自守形式与迹函数的相关性界限。

Method: 采用解析数论中的自守形式理论、迹函数方法以及相关和估计技术，处理复合模数情况下的复杂性。

Result: 得到了GL(3,Q)上自守系数与复合模迹函数相关和的界限估计。

Conclusion: 成功将前期工作中关于简单模的结果推广到复合模情形，为更一般的自守L函数研究提供了工具。

Abstract: We study bounds for correlation sums of automorphic coefficients on $\mathrm{GL}_{3,\mathbb{Q}}$ with trace functions of composite moduli. This is a sequel to our previous works with E. Kowalski and W. Sawin.

</details>


### [109] [Asymptotic Statistics of Odd Unimodal Sequences: Rank Distributions and Probabilistic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06459)
*Bing He,Guanting Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了奇数单峰序列（所有部分均为奇数的单峰序列）的统计性质，建立了秩统计量和形状参数的渐近分布理论，证明其秩统计量经标准化后收敛于双曲正割分布，并揭示了峰位、最大子部分等参数的极限行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将整数分拆的统计理论研究从经典情形拓展到奇数部分约束下的单峰序列，探索这一自然细化结构是否具有类似于经典分拆理论的概率结构，同时揭示奇数约束带来的新特征。

Method: 综合运用模变换理论、假theta函数渐近分析和条件Boltzmann模型，将此前用于单峰序列的概率工具推广到更具解析难度的奇数约束设定中。

Result: 建立了奇数单峰序列秩统计量的渐近分布，证明其经适当标准化后收敛于双曲正割分布；同时得到了峰位、峰两侧最大子部分以及小部分联合行为的极限分布。

Conclusion: 奇数单峰序列展现出与经典整数分拆理论平行的丰富概率结构，同时因奇数约束而呈现独特的分布特征；提出的统一方法桥接了模形式理论与概率论。

Abstract: Integer partitions have fascinated people for centuries, from Ramanujan's groundbreaking congruences to the modern theory of modular forms. This paper investigates the statistical properties of odd unimodal sequences--a natural refinement where sequences rise to a peak and then fall, but with the constraint that all parts must be odd, and develops a comprehensive statistical theory for their rank and shape parameters. We establish the asymptotic distribution of the rank statistic and demonstrate that, when properly normalized, it converges to the hyperbolic secant distribution. Beyond the rank distribution, limiting distributions of the peak, the largest parts on either side of the peak, and the joint behavior of small parts are also proved. These results reveal a rich probabilistic structure that parallels the classical theory of integer partitions while exhibiting distinctive new features arising from the odd-part constraint. The analysis employs a synthesis of modular transformation theory, false theta function asymptotics, and conditioned Boltzmann models. This extends the probabilistic machinery previously developed for unimodal sequences into a more general and analytically demanding setting, offering a unified approach that bridges modular forms and probability.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Kernel of Scott modules and Brauer indecomposability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05757)
*Lin Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了特征p代数闭域上有限群G的Scott kG模的Brauer不可分解性，通过推广判断准则，并证明在某些情况下这种性质可以从p-局部子群的Scott模提升而来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Scott模的Brauer不可分解性，并推广其判断准则，同时探索该性质能否从p-局部子群提升到整个群。

Method: 通过分析模的核与Brauer不可分解性之间的关系，推广已有准则，并建立从p-局部子群到整个群的提升定理。

Result: 推广了Scott kG模的Brauer不可分解性的判断准则，并证明了在某些条件下该性质可以从p-局部子群的Scott模提升而来。

Conclusion: 本文为判断有限群Scott模的Brauer不可分解性提供了更广泛的标准，并揭示了其与局部子群之间的关系。

Abstract: Let $k$ be an algebraically closed field of a prime characteristic $p$. Let $G$ be a finite group. We investigate the Brauer indecomposability of Scott $kG$-modules in relation to the kernel of modules. We generalize a criterion for Brauer indecomposability. We also prove that, in certain cases, Brauer indecomposability of a Scott $kG$-module can be lifted from that of a Scott module over a $p$-local subgroup.

</details>


### [111] [Distribution of spin norm along pencils: the $Sp(p, q)$ case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06015)
*Chao-Ping Dong,Zhan Ying*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对 $Sp(p,q)$ 群而言，一旦沿着 Vogan 铅笔越过酉小凸包，自旋范数严格递增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续 [2] 和 [3] 中定理 C 的工作，进一步研究 $Sp(p,q)$ 群上 Vogan 铅笔中自旋范数的单调性。

Method: 基于之前的工作，分析 $Sp(p,q)$ 群的 Vogan 铅笔结构，并研究自旋范数在越过酉小凸包后的行为。

Result: 自旋范数在 Vogan 铅笔越过酉小凸包后严格递增。

Conclusion: 该结果完善了对 $Sp(p,q)$ 群上 Vogan 铅笔与自旋范数关系的理解。

Abstract: As a sequel to [2] and Theorem C of [3], this paper shows that for $Sp(p,q)$, the spin norm strictly increases along any Vogan pencil once it goes beyond the unitarily small convex hull.

</details>


### [112] [A Sugawara-Legendre mechanism for the hyperelliptic Heisenberg algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06090)
*Felipe Albino dos Santos*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究超椭圆曲线情形下Heisenberg子代数$\mathcal{H}_2$的$\varphi$-Verma模，证明了Shapovalov型在Legendre多项式基下对角化、模不可约性等价于$p$-可容许性、以及存在一个将Sugawara零模映到经典Legendre微分算子的显式交织子，从而将模论与正交多项式家族联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超椭圆曲线$u^2 = P(t)$对应的Heisenberg子代数$\mathcal{H}_m$的$\varphi$-Verma模中出现的正交多项式族如何受模论控制，建立表示论与特殊函数之间的深层联系。

Method: 首先构造$\varphi$-Verma模$M(\varphi)$及其上的反变形式；然后证明该形式在由2-cocycle确定的多项式基$\{\tilde{P}_n\}$下是对角化的，且平方范数为$2/(2n+1)$；进而分析模的不可约性与Shapovalov型非退化性的等价条件；最后构造从$M(\varphi)$到$\mathbb{C}[x]$的显式交织子$\Phi$，将Sugawara零模$\Omega$映到Legendre微分算子$L$，最高权向量映到Legendre多项式。

Result: (1) Shapovalov型在多项式基$\{\tilde{P}_n\}$下对角化，Legendre平方范数$h_n=2/(2n+1)$；(2) $M(\varphi)$不可约当且仅当$\varphi$是$p$-可容许的，等价于Shapovalov型非退化；(3) 存在显式交织子将$\Omega$映到$L$、最高权向量映到$P_n(x)$、Casimir塔$\{\Omega^r\}$映到$\{L^r\}$；(4) $M(\varphi)$与玻色Fock空间典范同构，Shapovalov型与Fock内积一致。

Conclusion: 在超椭圆情形$m=2$下完整建立了Heisenberg子代数$\mathcal{H}_2$的Verma模理论与Legendre多项式（及Legendre微分算子）之间的精确对应，为$m\ge 3$的更一般情形提出了猜想。

Abstract: We study the $\varphi$-Verma modules of the Heisenberg subalgebra $\mathcal{H}_m$ of the universal central extension of $\mathfrak{sl}_2 \otimes A_m$, where $A_m$ is the coordinate ring of the superelliptic curve $u^m = P(t)$, and ask how the orthogonal polynomial families that arise in the centre relations are controlled by the module theory of $\mathcal{H}_m$. Our main results are proved unconditionally for the hyperelliptic case $m=2$, $r=1$; corresponding statements for $m \ge 3$ are recorded as conjectures. In the hyperelliptic case we prove three theorems. First, the canonical contravariant (Shapovalov) form on $M(\varphi)$ is diagonal in the polynomial basis $\{\tilde{P}_n\}_{n \ge 0}$ determined by the cocycle, with Legendre squared norms $h_n = 2/(2n+1)$. Second, $M(\varphi)$ is irreducible if and only if $\varphi$ is $p$-admissible, and this is equivalent to non-degeneracy of the Shapovalov form. Third, there is an explicit intertwiner $Φ\colon M(\varphi) \to \mathbb{C}[x]$ which sends the free-boson Sugawara zero mode $Ω= -L_0(L_0 + \mathrm{Id}) \in \widetilde{U(\mathcal{H}_m)}$ to the classical Legendre differential operator $L = (1-x^2)\partial_x^2 - 2x\partial_x$, the level-$n$ image of the highest-weight vector to the Legendre polynomial $P_n(x)$, and the Casimir tower $\{Ω^r\}_{r \ge 1}$ to $\{L^r\}_{r \ge 1}$. As a companion result, $M(\varphi)$ is canonically isomorphic to a bosonic Fock space with the Shapovalov form identified with the Fock inner product.

</details>


### [113] [A Microlocal Description of Aubert-Zelevinsky Duality on Unipotent $L$-Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06180)
*Jonas Antor,Emile Okada*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了所有内形式简单伴随非分歧p-adic群的幺幂表示的Aubert-Zelevinsky对合的微局域描述。通过将增强L-参数实现为反常层，证明该对合对应傅里叶变换、对偶群紧形式带来的复共轭映射、以及无穷小参数紧部分上的逆这三个操作的复合。当群非D4三重态形式的内形式时，该对合简化为傅里叶变换、Chevalley对合和局部系统对偶的复合。此外，作为结果证明了幺幂A-参数的微局域Hiraga猜想。为统一证明，重新阐述并澄清了幺幂局部Langlands对应的构造，证明了graded Hecke代数的"典范"同构，并证明了几何graded Hecke代数的单模由p-adic群对应表示的某些合成重数唯一确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Aubert-Zelevinsky对合在幺幂表示上的描述仅在某些特殊例子中被部分观察到（仅涉及Chevalley对合和傅里叶变换的贡献），缺乏统一完整的微局域描述；同时Hiraga猜想在幺幂A-参数情形尚未证明。需要建立一套统一的理论框架。

Method: 利用增强L-参数的反常层实现，将Aubert-Zelevinsky对合重新解释为傅里叶变换、复共轭映射和紧部分逆的复合。重新阐述并澄清幺幂局部Langlands对应的构造，证明graded Hecke代数的唯一典范同构，通过该同构建立单模的合成重数刻画。

Result: 给出了Aubert-Zelevinsky对合的完整微局域描述；在非D4三重态情形简化为三项操作；证明了幺幂A-参数的微局域Hiraga猜想；建立了graded Hecke代数之间唯一典范同构；证明了几何graded Hecke代数的单模由p-adic群表示的合成重数唯一确定。

Conclusion: 本文为幺幂表示的Aubert-Zelevinsky对合提供了统一的微局域描述，澄清了此前仅观察到部分贡献的原因（局部系统对偶在自对偶情形不可见），并推广了Hiraga猜想的结果。对幺幂局部Langlands对应的构造进行了统一和澄清。

Abstract: We give a microlocal description of the Aubert--Zelevinsky involution for all unipotent representations of all inner forms of simple adjoint unramified $p$-adic groups. Via the realization of enhanced $L$-parameters as perverse sheaves, we show that the involution corresponds to the composition of three operations: Fourier transform, the complex conjugation map coming from the compact form of the dual group, and inversion on the compact part of the infinitesimal parameter.
 We also show that when the group is not inner to a triality form of $D_4$, this simplifies to the composition of Fourier transform, Chevalley involution, and duality on local systems. This was previously verified in certain special examples by several authors where only the contribution by Chevalley involution and Fourier transform was observed. Duality on local systems is invisible in these examples since they only involve self-dual local systems.
 Finally, we prove the microlocal Hiraga conjecture for unipotent $A$-parameters of inner-to-split simple adjoint groups as a consequence of our results.
 In order to give a uniform proof of our results we reformulate and clarify several aspects of the construction of the unipotent local Langlands correspondence. This additionally allows us to characterize how various affine and graded Hecke algebras are identified. We prove that there is a `canonical' way to do so by showing that there is a unique isomorphism of graded Hecke algebras compatible with the Kottwitz isomorphism. As an application of this, we show that a simple module of the geometric graded Hecke algebra is uniquely determined by certain composition multiplicities coming from the corresponding representation of the $p$-adic group. This can be understood as a characterization of the unipotent local Langlands correspondence.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [A Constant-Factor Approximation for Continuous Dynamic Time Warping in 2D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05917)
*Kevin Buchin,Maike Buchin,Jan Erik Swiadek,Sampson Wong*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对二维连续动态时间规整（CDTW）的算法复杂度问题，首次提出了在1-范数下运行时间O(n⁵)的5-近似算法，并将其推广到所有多边形范数，进而实现了任意固定范数下(5+ε)-近似且时间复杂度为O(n⁵/ε^{1/2})的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管连续动态时间规整（CDTW）作为多边形曲线的鲁棒相似性度量已有广泛应用，但二维情况下其精确解或强近似保证的算法复杂度尚不明确，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出一个在1-范数下运行时间O(n⁵)的5-近似算法；随后将算法推广到ℝ²上所有多边形范数，并进一步在任意固定范数下实现(5+ε)-近似，时间复杂度为O(n⁵/ε^{1/2})。

Result: 首次实现了二维CDTW的多项式时间常数因子近似算法（5-近似），并扩展至包括欧几里得2-范数在内的任意固定范数下的(5+ε)-近似。

Conclusion: 填补了二维CDTW算法复杂度理论分析的空白，为实际应用提供了理论保证和高效近似算法。

Abstract: Continuous Dynamic Time Warping (CDTW) is a robust similarity measure for polygonal curves that has recently found a variety of applications. Despite its practical use, not much is known about the algorithmic complexity of computing it in 2D, especially when one requires either an exact solution or strong approximation guarantees. We fill this gap by introducing a $5$-approximation algorithm with running time $O(n^5)$ under the 1-norm.
  This is the first constant-factor approximation for 2D CDTW with polynomial running time. We extend our algorithm to all polygonal norms on $\mathbb{R}^2$, which we subsequently use in order to achieve a $(5+\varepsilon)$-approximation with time complexity $O(n^5 / \varepsilon^{1/2})$ for CDTW in 2D under any fixed norm. The latter result in particular includes the usual Euclidean 2-norm.

</details>


### [115] [Scalable GPU Construction of 3D Voronoi and Power Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06408)
*Bernardo Taveira,Carl Lindström,Maryam Fatemi,Lars Hammarstrand,Fredrik Kahl*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高度可并行的GPU算法，用于快速构建大规模3D Voronoi图和Power图。该方法通过渐进式裁剪初始单元体积，并结合基于方向几何边界的剔除准则与层次化最佳优先遍历，实现了对任意空间分布的大规模点集的高效处理，在性能上媲美现有最优Delaunay四面体化方法，并自然推广到Power图场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D Voronoi图构建方法存在计算开销大、难以扩展到大规模点集的问题。CPU方法依赖Delaunay四面体化，在大规模场景下速度过慢；GPU方法要么难以高效扩展到大规模点集，要么假设点分布均匀。对于更一般的加权Power图，现有方法通常针对特定应用定制，假设均匀分布和小的权重变化，无法适用于复杂的异构数据。

Method: 提出一种高度可并行的GPU算法，通过渐进式裁剪初始单元体积（从加权3D点出发，逐步用候选邻近点诱导的平分平面进行裁剪）来构建每个凸胞。为高效识别任意空间分布下的候选邻近点，引入基于演化胞方向几何边界的剔除准则，并结合包围体层次结构的最佳优先遍历策略。

Result: 该方法在中等问题规模上性能与当前最优的Delaunay四面体化方法相当，同时展现出对大规模点集和多样化空间分布的鲁棒可扩展性，并且无需额外假设即可自然推广到Power图。

Conclusion: 该工作为大规模3D Voronoi图和Power图的快速GPU构建提供了一种通用高效的解决方案，克服了现有方法在可扩展性和分布假设方面的局限性，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Voronoi diagrams, and their more general weighted counterpart, power diagrams, are fundamental geometric constructs with wide-ranging applications. Recently, they have gained renewed attention in mesh-based neural rendering. Despite being extensively studied, the construction of 3D Voronoi diagrams for large-scale point sets remains computationally expensive, limiting their adoption in large-scale applications. Existing CPU-based approaches typically rely on computing its dual, the Delaunay tetrahedralization, but are prohibitively slow for large diagrams, while GPU-based methods either struggle to scale efficiently to large point sets or assume homogeneous point distributions. The weighted case, power diagrams, is even less explored in this context. Existing approaches are typically tailored to the application at hand, assuming homogeneous point distributions and small weight variations, making them unsuitable for general use in more complex heterogeneous data.
  In this paper, we present a highly parallelizable GPU algorithm for the fast construction of large-scale 3D Voronoi and power diagrams. Our approach constructs each convex cell from a weighted 3D point by progressively clipping an initial cell volume against bisecting planes induced by candidate neighboring points. To efficiently identify candidate neighbors under arbitrary spatial distributions, we introduce a culling criterion based on directional geometric bounds of the evolving cell, combined with a hierarchical best-first traversal of bounding volumes.
  We achieve performance on par with state-of-the-art Delaunay tetrahedralization methods on small and moderate problem sizes, while exhibiting robust scalability to large point sets and diverse spatial distributions. Moreover, our method naturally generalizes to power diagrams without additional assumptions. See https://research.zenseact.com/publications/paragram .

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究评估了冰立方升级项目对暗物质湮灭产生的中微子通量的探测灵敏度，结果表明仅需三年数据即可在3-500 GeV质量范围内对部分暗物质模型实现领先限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰立方升级项目旨在提升1-500 GeV能量范围内中微子的探测能力，从而改善对低质量暗物质场景的灵敏度。

Method: 通过模拟计算，评估冰立方升级对来自太阳核心和银河系中心的暗物质湮灭产生的中微子通量的探测灵敏度。

Result: 冰立方升级将在3-500 GeV质量范围内对暗物质实现严格限制，仅需三年数据即可在某些暗物质模型上达到领先灵敏度。

Conclusion: 冰立方升级项目将显著提升对低质量暗物质的探测能力，有望在相关参数空间中取得领先成果。

Abstract: The IceCube Upgrade, an extension designed to enhance the IceCube Neutrino Observatory's detection of neutrinos with energies between 1 GeV and 500 GeV, will markedly improve IceCube's sensitivity to low-mass dark matter scenarios. In this study, we present sensitivity projections for the IceCube Upgrade to neutrino fluxes arising from dark matter annihilation. In particular, we consider dark matter with masses between 3 GeV to 500 GeV from both the core of the Sun and the Galactic Center. These projections indicate that the IceCube Upgrade will enable stringent limits on dark matter in this parameter space, achieving leading sensitivities to some dark matter models with only three years of data taking.

</details>
